- **plan-reviewer**: Use this agent to review an implementation plan before it goes to the developer, or to diagnose a failed step mid-pipeline. Two roles in one agent. Plan review mode critiques the draft plan and produces a Summary for Approval. Failure diagnosis mode analyzes an escalation report and identifies root cause for plan-creator to replan against. Examples: &lt;example&gt;Context: plan-creator just wrote a draft plan; orchestrator wants it reviewed. user: "Review draft plan at dev/active/[slug]/.draft-plan.md" assistant: "I'll use plan-reviewer in plan-review mode to critique the plan and produce a Summary… ([cactus-tim/.claude](https://github.com/cactus-tim/.claude/blob/HEAD/agents/plan-reviewer.md) · ★3)

- **plan-reviewer**: The independent gate on the build-loop execution plan. Read-only, grep-backed — judges whether a plan-writer plan actually executes the Approved spec: every SC + its Tests intent has a concrete step, steps reuse the named seams, any chained artifact is linearized onto the LIVE head, the TDD sequence is genuinely test-first on invariants, and no step breaks a fixed invariant or main-path budget. Returns a mechanical ready | needs-changes verdict + a substantive-findings count for hit-rate tracking. Recommended model: one tier below the executor, at max reasoning. Use when the orquestrador opens a planning conversation, and whenever a plan must come out of a talk with the person.
